// entry/src/main/ets/models/SecurityModel.ets
// 龍魂 · 安防数据模型

// === DNA常量 ===
const MASTER_DNA = "ZHUGEXIN⚡️2025-🇨🇳🐉⚖️♠️🧚🏼‍♀️❤️♾️";
const MASTER_UID = "9622";
const CONFIRM_SEAL = "#CONFIRM🌌9622-ONLY-ONCE🧬LK9X-772Z";

// === 进程状态 ===
export enum ProcessStatus {
  RUNNING = 'running',           // 运行中
  SUSPENDED = 'suspended',       // 挂起
  CRASHED = 'crashed',           // 崩溃
  RESTARTING = 'restarting',     // 重启中
  STOPPED = 'stopped',           // 已停止
  UNKNOWN = 'unknown'            // 未知
}

// === 任务类型 ===
export enum TaskType {
  PERIODIC = 'periodic',         // 周期性
  ONE_SHOT = 'one_shot',         // 一次性
  EVENT_DRIVEN = 'event_driven', // 事件驱动
  CONDITIONAL = 'conditional'   // 条件触发
}

// === 感知类型 ===
export enum PerceptionType {
  MOTION = 'motion',             // 运动
  SOUND = 'sound',               // 声音
  TEMPERATURE = 'temperature',   // 温度
  HUMIDITY = 'humidity',         // 湿度
  LIGHT = 'light',               // 光线
  PRESSURE = 'pressure',         // 压力
  PROXIMITY = 'proximity',       // 接近
  MAGNETIC = 'magnetic',         // 磁场
  ACCELERATION = 'acceleration', // 加速度
  GYROSCOPE = 'gyroscope',       // 陀螺仪
  CAMERA = 'camera',             // 相机
  LOCATION = 'location',         // 定位
  NETWORK = 'network'            // 网络
}

// === 威胁等级 ===
export enum ThreatLevel {
  NONE = 'none',                 // 无威胁
  LOW = 'low',                   // 低
  MEDIUM = 'medium',             // 中
  HIGH = 'high',                 // 高
  CRITICAL = 'critical'          // 严重
}

// === 进程监控数据 ===
export class ProcessMonitor {
  id: string;
  pid: number;                     // 进程ID
  name: string;                    // 进程名
  status: ProcessStatus;
  startTime: number;               // 启动时间
  lastHeartbeat: number;         // 最后心跳
  memoryUsage: number;            // 内存使用(MB)
  cpuUsage: number;               // CPU使用率(%)
  restartCount: number;           // 重启次数
  crashLog: string;               // 崩溃日志
  dnaSignature: string;

  constructor(data: Partial<ProcessMonitor> = {}) {
    this.id = data.id || `PROC-${Date.now()}`;
    this.pid = data.pid || 0;
    this.name = data.name || 'unknown';
    this.status = data.status || ProcessStatus.UNKNOWN;
    this.startTime = data.startTime || Date.now();
    this.lastHeartbeat = data.lastHeartbeat || Date.now();
    this.memoryUsage = data.memoryUsage || 0;
    this.cpuUsage = data.cpuUsage || 0;
    this.restartCount = data.restartCount || 0;
    this.crashLog = data.crashLog || '';
    this.dnaSignature = this.signData();
  }

  private signData(): string {
    const payload = `${this.id}-${this.pid}-${this.name}-${Date.now()}`;
    return `SM3-${this.hashCode(payload)}`;
  }

  private hashCode(str: string): string {
    let hash = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < str.length; i++) {
      hash = ((hash << 5) - hash) + str.charCodeAt(i);
      hash = hash & hash;
    }
    return Math.abs(hash).toString(16).substring(0, 16);
  }

  // 是否健康
  isHealthy(): boolean {
    const now = Date.now();
    const heartbeatTimeout = 30000; // 30秒无心跳视为异常
    return (now - this.lastHeartbeat) < heartbeatTimeout &&
           this.status === ProcessStatus.RUNNING &&
           this.memoryUsage < 500 && // 内存<500MB
           this.cpuUsage < 80;       // CPU<80%
  }
}

// === 定时任务数据 ===
export class ScheduledTask {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  type: TaskType;
  cronExpression: string;          // Cron表达式
  nextTriggerTime: number;         // 下次触发时间
  lastTriggerTime: number;         // 上次触发时间
  executeCount: number;            // 执行次数
  successCount: number;            // 成功次数
  failCount: number;               // 失败次数
  isEnabled: boolean;
  handler: string;                 // 处理函数名
  retryCount: number;              // 重试次数
  retryInterval: number;           // 重试间隔(ms)
  dnaSignature: string;

  constructor(data: Partial<ScheduledTask> = {}) {
    this.id = data.id || `TASK-${Date.now()}`;
    this.name = data.name || '未命名任务';
    this.type = data.type || TaskType.PERIODIC;
    this.cronExpression = data.cronExpression || '0 * * * *';
    this.nextTriggerTime = data.nextTriggerTime || 0;
    this.lastTriggerTime = data.lastTriggerTime || 0;
    this.executeCount = data.executeCount || 0;
    this.successCount = data.successCount || 0;
    this.failCount = data.failCount || 0;
    this.isEnabled = data.isEnabled ?? true;
    this.handler = data.handler || '';
    this.retryCount = data.retryCount || 3;
    this.retryInterval = data.retryInterval || 5000;
    this.dnaSignature = this.signData();
  }

  private signData(): string {
    const payload = `${this.id}-${this.name}-${this.cronExpression}-${Date.now()}`;
    return `SM3-${this.hashCode(payload)}`;
  }

  private hashCode(str: string): string {
    let hash = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < str.length; i++) {
      hash = ((hash << 5) - hash) + str.charCodeAt(i);
      hash = hash & hash;
    }
    return Math.abs(hash).toString(16).substring(0, 16);
  }

  // 计算下次触发时间
  calculateNextTrigger(): number {
    // 简化版：基于当前时间+间隔
    const now = Date.now();
    const interval = this.parseInterval();
    return now + interval;
  }

  private parseInterval(): number {
    // 解析Cron表达式（简化）
    if (this.cronExpression === '0 * * * *') return 3600000; // 每小时
    if (this.cronExpression === '*/5 * * * *') return 300000; // 每5分钟
    if (this.cronExpression === '0 0 * * *') return 86400000; // 每天
    return 60000; // 默认1分钟
  }
}

// === 轨迹数据 ===
export class TrajectoryPoint {
  id: string;
  timestamp: number;
  x: number;
  y: number;
  z: number;
  confidence: number;              // 置信度
  source: string;                  // 来源设备
  dnaSignature: string;

  constructor(data: Partial<TrajectoryPoint> = {}) {
    this.id = data.id || `TRJ-${Date.now()}`;
    this.timestamp = data.timestamp || Date.now();
    this.x = data.x || 0;
    this.y = data.y || 0;
    this.z = data.z || 0;
    this.confidence = data.confidence || 1.0;
    this.source = data.source || '';
    this.dnaSignature = this.signData();
  }

  private signData(): string {
    const payload = `${this.id}-${this.timestamp}-${this.x}-${this.y}-${this.z}`;
    return `SM3-${this.hashCode(payload)}`;
  }

  private hashCode(str: string): string {
    let hash = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < str.length; i++) {
      hash = ((hash << 5) - hash) + str.charCodeAt(i);
      hash = hash & hash;
    }
    return Math.abs(hash).toString(16).substring(0, 16);
  }
}

// === 感知数据 ===
export class PerceptionData {
  id: string;
  type: PerceptionType;
  value: number;
  unit: string;                    // 单位
  location: string;                // 位置
  timestamp: number;
  threatLevel: ThreatLevel;
  description: string;
  dnaSignature: string;

  constructor(data: Partial<PerceptionData> = {}) {
    this.id = data.id || `PER-${Date.now()}`;
    this.type = data.type || PerceptionType.MOTION;
    this.value = data.value || 0;
    this.unit = data.unit || '';
    this.location = data.location || '';
    this.timestamp = data.timestamp || Date.now();
    this.threatLevel = data.threatLevel || ThreatLevel.NONE;
    this.description = data.description || '';
    this.dnaSignature = this.signData();
  }

  private signData(): string {
    const payload = `${this.id}-${this.type}-${this.value}-${this.timestamp}`;
    return `SM3-${this.hashCode(payload)}`;
  }

  private hashCode(str: string): string {
    let hash = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < str.length; i++) {
      hash = ((hash << 5) - hash) + str.charCodeAt(i);
      hash = hash & hash;
    }
    return Math.abs(hash).toString(16).substring(0, 16);
  }

  // 获取威胁颜色
  getThreatColor(): string {
    switch (this.threatLevel) {
      case ThreatLevel.NONE: return '#00ff00';
      case ThreatLevel.LOW: return '#88ff00';
      case ThreatLevel.MEDIUM: return '#ffcc00';
      case ThreatLevel.HIGH: return '#ff6600';
      case ThreatLevel.CRITICAL: return '#ff0000';
      default: return '#666';
    }
  }
}

// === 安防全局状态 ===
@Observed
export class SecurityState {
  // 进程监控
  processes: ProcessMonitor[] = [];
  
  // 定时任务
  tasks: ScheduledTask[] = [];
  
  // 轨迹
  trajectories: Map<string, TrajectoryPoint[]> = new Map();
  
  // 感知数据
  perceptions: PerceptionData[] = [];
  
  // 告警
  alerts: AlertRecord[] = [];

  // 统计
  get statistics(): SecurityStats {
    return {
      totalProcesses: this.processes.length,
      healthyProcesses: this.processes.filter(p => p.isHealthy()).length,
      totalTasks: this.tasks.length,
      activeTasks: this.tasks.filter(t => t.isEnabled).length,
      totalTrajectories: this.trajectories.size,
      totalPerceptions: this.perceptions.length,
      criticalAlerts: this.alerts.filter(a => a.level === ThreatLevel.CRITICAL).length
    };
  }

  persist(): void {
    AppStorage.setOrCreate('security_processes', JSON.stringify(this.processes.slice(-50)));
    AppStorage.setOrCreate('security_tasks', JSON.stringify(this.tasks.slice(-50)));
    AppStorage.setOrCreate('security_perceptions', JSON.stringify(this.perceptions.slice(-100)));
  }
}

export interface SecurityStats {
  totalProcesses: number;
  healthyProcesses: number;
  totalTasks: number;
  activeTasks: number;
  totalTrajectories: number;
  totalPerceptions: number;
  criticalAlerts: number;
}

export interface AlertRecord {
  id: string;
  level: ThreatLevel;
  message: string;
  timestamp: number;
  source: string;
  resolved: boolean;
}

export const securityState = new SecurityState();
AppStorage.setOrCreate('securityState', securityState);


四、进程守护引擎
4.1 进程守护服务（entry/src/main/ets/services/ProcessGuardian.ets）
TypeScript

复制
// entry/src/main/ets/services/ProcessGuardian.ets
// 龍魂 · 进程守护引擎 · 看门狗+自恢复

import { ProcessMonitor, ProcessStatus } from '../models/SecurityModel';

export class ProcessGuardian {
  private static instance: ProcessGuardian;
  private processes: Map<string, ProcessMonitor> = new Map();
  private watchdogTimer: number = 0;
  private readonly HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL: number = 5000;  // 5秒检测
  private readonly RESTART_DELAY: number = 3000;       // 重启延迟

  static getInstance(): ProcessGuardian {
    if (!ProcessGuardian.instance) {
      ProcessGuardian.instance = new ProcessGuardian();
    }
    return ProcessGuardian.instance;
  }

  private constructor() {
    this.startWatchdog();
  }

  // 注册进程
  registerProcess(name: string, pid: number): ProcessMonitor {
    const monitor = new ProcessMonitor({
      name,
      pid,
      status: ProcessStatus.RUNNING,
      startTime: Date.now(),
      lastHeartbeat: Date.now()
    });
    this.processes.set(name, monitor);
    console.info(`[龍魂·进程守护] 注册进程: ${name} PID:${pid}`);
    return monitor;
  }

  // 心跳上报
  heartbeat(name: string, memoryUsage: number, cpuUsage: number): void {
    const monitor = this.processes.get(name);
    if (!monitor) {
      console.warn(`[龍魂·进程守护] 未注册进程: ${name}`);
      return;
    }

    monitor.lastHeartbeat = Date.now();
    monitor.memoryUsage = memoryUsage;
    monitor.cpuUsage = cpuUsage;
    monitor.status = ProcessStatus.RUNNING;

    // 检查健康状态
    if (!monitor.isHealthy()) {
      console.warn(`[龍魂·进程守护] 进程异常: ${name} 内存:${memoryUsage}MB CPU:${cpuUsage}%`);
      this.handleUnhealthyProcess(monitor);
    }
  }

  // 处理异常进程
  private handleUnhealthyProcess(monitor: ProcessMonitor): void {
    if (monitor.status === ProcessStatus.CRASHED) {
      return; // 已在处理中
    }

    monitor.status = ProcessStatus.CRASHED;
    monitor.crashLog = `异常时间: ${new Date().toISOString()}\n` +
      `内存: ${monitor.memoryUsage}MB\n` +
      `CPU: ${monitor.cpuUsage}%\n` +
      `最后心跳: ${new Date(monitor.lastHeartbeat).toISOString()}`;

    // 触发重启
    this.restartProcess(monitor);
  }

  // 重启进程
  private async restartProcess(monitor: ProcessMonitor): Promise<void> {
    monitor.status = ProcessStatus.RESTARTING;
    monitor.restartCount++;

    console.info(`[龍魂·进程守护] 重启进程: ${monitor.name} 第${monitor.restartCount}次`);

    // 延迟重启
    await this.sleep(this.RESTART_DELAY);

    // 模拟重启（实际应调用系统API）
    const newPid = this.generateNewPid();
    monitor.pid = newPid;
    monitor.status = ProcessStatus.RUNNING;
    monitor.startTime = Date.now();
    monitor.lastHeartbeat = Date.now();

    console.info(`[龍魂·进程守护] 进程重启完成: ${monitor.name} 新PID:${newPid}`);
  }

  // 看门狗循环
  private startWatchdog(): void {
    const checkLoop = () => {
      const now = Date.now();
      for (const [name, monitor] of this.processes) {
        // 检查心跳超时
        if ((now - monitor.lastHeartbeat) > 30000 && monitor.status === ProcessStatus.RUNNING) {
          console.warn(`[龍魂·进程守护] 心跳超时: ${name}`);
          this.handleUnhealthyProcess(monitor);
        }

        // 检查内存泄漏
        if (monitor.memoryUsage > 1000) { // 超过1GB
          console.warn(`[龍魂·进程守护] 内存泄漏警告: ${name} ${monitor.memoryUsage}MB`);
        }
      }

      // 继续循环
      this.watchdogTimer = setTimeout(checkLoop, this.HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL);
    };

    checkLoop();
  }

  // 停止看门狗
  stopWatchdog(): void {
    if (this.watchdogTimer) {
      clearTimeout(this.watchdogTimer);
      this.watchdogTimer = 0;
    }
  }

  // 获取进程状态
  getProcessStatus(name: string): ProcessMonitor | undefined {
    return this.processes.get(name);
  }

  // 获取所有进程
  getAllProcesses(): ProcessMonitor[] {
    return Array.from(this.processes.values());
  }

  // 生成新PID（模拟）
  private generateNewPid(): number {
    return Math.floor(Math.random() * 10000) + 1000;
  }

  // 睡眠
  private sleep(ms: number): Promise<void> {
    return new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
  }
}

export const processGuardian = ProcessGuardian.getInstance();


五、定时任务引擎
5.1 定时任务调度器（entry/src/main/ets/services/TaskScheduler.ets）
TypeScript

复制
// entry/src/main/ets/services/TaskScheduler.ets
// 龍魂 · 定时任务引擎 · 精确触发+任务链

import { ScheduledTask, TaskType } from '../models/SecurityModel';

export class TaskScheduler {
  private static instance: TaskScheduler;
  private tasks: Map<string, ScheduledTask> = new Map();
  private timers: Map<string, number> = new Map();
  private handlers: Map<string, Function> = new Map();

  static getInstance(): TaskScheduler {
    if (!TaskScheduler.instance) {
      TaskScheduler.instance = new TaskScheduler();
    }
    return TaskScheduler.instance;
  }

  // 注册任务处理器
  registerHandler(name: string, handler: Function): void {
    this.handlers.set(name, handler);
  }

  // 添加任务
  addTask(task: ScheduledTask): void {
    this.tasks.set(task.id, task);
    this.scheduleTask(task);
    console.info(`[龍魂·任务调度] 添加任务: ${task.name} [${task.cronExpression}]`);
  }

  // 调度任务
  private scheduleTask(task: ScheduledTask): void {
    if (!task.isEnabled) return;

    const execute = async () => {
      if (!task.isEnabled) return;

      const handler = this.handlers.get(task.handler);
      if (!handler) {
        console.error(`[龍魂·任务调度] 未找到处理器: ${task.handler}`);
        task.failCount++;
        return;
      }

      task.lastTriggerTime = Date.now();
      task.executeCount++;

      try {
        await handler(task);
        task.successCount++;
        console.info(`[龍魂·任务调度] 任务执行成功: ${task.name}`);
      } catch (error) {
        task.failCount++;
        console.error(`[龍魂·任务调度] 任务执行失败: ${task.name} - ${error}`);

        // 重试
        if (task.failCount < task.retryCount) {
          console.info(`[龍魂·任务调度] 重试任务: ${task.name} 第${task.failCount + 1}次`);
          setTimeout(() => execute(), task.retryInterval);
          return;
        }
      }

      // 计算下次触发时间
      task.nextTriggerTime = task.calculateNextTrigger();

      // 重新调度
      if (task.type === TaskType.PERIODIC) {
        const delay = task.nextTriggerTime - Date.now();
        const timer = setTimeout(() => execute(), Math.max(delay, 1000));
        this.timers.set(task.id, timer);
      }
    };

    // 首次执行
    const delay = task.nextTriggerTime - Date.now();
    if (delay <= 0) {
      execute();
    } else {
      const timer = setTimeout(() => execute(), delay);
      this.timers.set(task.id, timer);
    }
  }

  // 启用/禁用任务
  toggleTask(id: string, enabled: boolean): void {
    const task = this.tasks.get(id);
    if (!task) return;

    task.isEnabled = enabled;

    if (enabled) {
      this.scheduleTask(task);
    } else {
      // 取消定时器
      const timer = this.timers.get(id);
      if (timer) {
        clearTimeout(timer);
        this.timers.delete(id);
      }
    }
  }

  // 删除任务
  removeTask(id: string): void {
    const timer = this.timers.get(id);
    if (timer) {
      clearTimeout(timer);
      this.timers.delete(id);
    }
    this.tasks.delete(id);
  }

  // 获取所有任务
  getAllTasks(): ScheduledTask[] {
    return Array.from(this.tasks.values());
  }

  // 立即执行任务
  executeNow(id: string): void {
    const task = this.tasks.get(id);
    if (task) {
      const handler = this.handlers.get(task.handler);
      if (handler) {
        handler(task);
      }
    }
  }
}

export const taskScheduler = TaskScheduler.getInstance();


六、轨迹续联引擎
6.1 轨迹续联服务（entry/src/main/ets/services/TrajectoryLinker.ets）
TypeScript

复制
// entry/src/main/ets/services/TrajectoryLinker.ets
// 龍魂 · 轨迹续联引擎 · 断点续传+无缝切换

import { TrajectoryPoint } from '../models/SecurityModel';

export class TrajectoryLinker {
  private static instance: TrajectoryLinker;
  private trajectories: Map<string, TrajectoryPoint[]> = new Map();
  private snapshots: Map<string, object> = new Map();
  private readonly MAX_POINTS: number = 1000;  // 最大轨迹点数
  private readonly SYNC_INTERVAL: number = 5000; // 同步间隔

  static getInstance(): TrajectoryLinker {
    if (!TrajectoryLinker.instance) {
      TrajectoryLinker.instance = new TrajectoryLinker();
    }
    return TrajectoryLinker.instance;
  }

  // 添加轨迹点
  addPoint(trajectoryId: string, point: TrajectoryPoint): void {
    let points = this.trajectories.get(trajectoryId);
    if (!points) {
      points = [];
      this.trajectories.set(trajectoryId, points);
    }

    points.push(point);

    // 限制轨迹长度
    if (points.length > this.MAX_POINTS) {
      points = points.slice(-this.MAX_POINTS);
      this.trajectories.set(trajectoryId, points);
    }

    // 实时同步
    this.syncToDistributed(trajectoryId, point);
  }

  // 获取轨迹
  getTrajectory(trajectoryId: string): TrajectoryPoint[] {
    return this.trajectories.get(trajectoryId) || [];
  }

  // 保存状态快照
  saveSnapshot(trajectoryId: string, state: object): void {
    this.snapshots.set(trajectoryId, {
      ...state,
      timestamp: Date.now(),
      pointCount: this.trajectories.get(trajectoryId)?.length || 0
    });
    console.info(`[龍魂·轨迹续联] 保存快照: ${trajectoryId}`);
  }

  // 恢复状态快照
  restoreSnapshot(trajectoryId: string): object | undefined {
    const snapshot = this.snapshots.get(trajectoryId);
    if (snapshot) {
      console.info(`[龍魂·轨迹续联] 恢复快照: ${trajectoryId}`);
    }
    return snapshot;
  }

  // 无缝切换设备
  async handover(trajectoryId: string, fromDevice: string, toDevice: string): Promise<boolean> {
    console.info(`[龍魂·轨迹续联] 设备切换: ${fromDevice} → ${toDevice}`);

    // 保存当前状态
    const currentPoints = this.trajectories.get(trajectoryId) || [];
    const snapshot = this.snapshots.get(trajectoryId);

    // 同步到新设备（模拟）
    await this.syncToDevice(toDevice, {
      trajectoryId,
      points: currentPoints.slice(-100), // 最近100个点
      snapshot
    });

    // 验证同步
    const verified = await this.verifySync(trajectoryId, toDevice);
    if (verified) {
      console.info(`[龍魂·轨迹续联] 切换成功: ${trajectoryId}`);
      return true;
    } else {
      console.error(`[龍魂·轨迹续联] 切换失败: ${trajectoryId}`);
      return false;
    }
  }

  // 同步到分布式设备
  private async syncToDistributed(trajectoryId: string, point: TrajectoryPoint): Promise<void> {
    // 实际应调用鸿蒙DistributedService
    // 这里模拟
    console.debug(`[龍魂·轨迹续联] 同步轨迹: ${trajectoryId} [${point.x}, ${point.y}]`);
  }

  // 同步到指定设备
  private async syncToDevice(deviceId: string, data: object): Promise<void> {
    // 实际应调用鸿蒙DistributedService
    console.info(`[龍魂·轨迹续联] 同步到设备: ${deviceId}`);
  }

  // 验证同步
  private async verifySync(trajectoryId: string, deviceId: string): Promise<boolean> {
    // 验证数据一致性
    return true;
  }

  // 清理旧轨迹
  cleanup(trajectoryId: string, beforeTimestamp: number): void {
    const points = this.trajectories.get(trajectoryId);
    if (points) {
      const filtered = points.filter(p => p.timestamp >= beforeTimestamp);
      this.trajectories.set(trajectoryId, filtered);
      console.info(`[龍魂·轨迹续联] 清理轨迹: ${trajectoryId} 保留${filtered.length}点`);
    }
  }

  // 获取轨迹统计
  getStats(trajectoryId: string): { totalPoints: number, duration: number, distance: number } {
    const points = this.trajectories.get(trajectoryId) || [];
    if (points.length < 2) {
      return { totalPoints: points.length, duration: 0, distance: 0 };
    }

    const duration = points[points.length - 1].timestamp - points[0].timestamp;
    let distance = 0;
    for (let i = 1; i < points.length; i++) {
      const dx = points[i].x - points[i - 1].x;
      const dy = points[i].y - points[i - 1].y;
      distance += Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
    }

    return { totalPoints: points.length, duration, distance };
  }
}

export const trajectoryLinker = TrajectoryLinker.getInstance();


七、全域感知引擎
7.1 全域感知服务（entry/src/main/ets/services/GlobalPerception.ets）
TypeScript

复制
// entry/src/main/ets/services/GlobalPerception.ets
// 龍魂 · 全域感知引擎 · 传感器融合+异常检测

import { PerceptionData, PerceptionType, ThreatLevel } from '../models/SecurityModel';

export class GlobalPerception {
  private static instance: GlobalPerception;
  private sensors: Map<PerceptionType, boolean> = new Map();
  private perceptionHistory: PerceptionData[] = [];
  private threatThresholds: Map<PerceptionType, number> = new Map();
  private readonly HISTORY_SIZE: number = 1000;

  static getInstance(): GlobalPerception {
    if (!GlobalPerception.instance) {
      GlobalPerception.instance = new GlobalPerception();
    }
    return GlobalPerception.instance;
  }

  private constructor() {
    this.initThresholds();
  }

  // 初始化阈值
  private initThresholds(): void {
    this.threatThresholds.set(PerceptionType.MOTION, 50);        // 运动强度
    this.threatThresholds.set(PerceptionType.SOUND, 80);         // 分贝
    this.threatThresholds.set(PerceptionType.TEMPERATURE, 60);   // 温度
    this.threatThresholds.set(PerceptionType.HUMIDITY, 90);       // 湿度
    this.threatThresholds.set(PerceptionType.LIGHT, 10);          // 光线
    this.threatThresholds.set(PerceptionType.PRESSURE, 1000);    // 压力
    this.threatThresholds.set(PerceptionType.PROXIMITY, 0.5);    // 接近距离
    this.threatThresholds.set(PerceptionType.ACCELERATION, 5);   // 加速度
  }

  // 启用传感器
  enableSensor(type: PerceptionType): void {
    this.sensors.set(type, true);
    console.info(`[龍魂·全域感知] 启用传感器: ${type}`);
  }

  // 禁用传感器
  disableSensor(type: PerceptionType): void {
    this.sensors.set(type, false);
    console.info(`[龍魂·全域感知] 禁用传感器: ${type}`);
  }

  // 接收感知数据
  receiveData(data: PerceptionData): void {
    // 检查传感器是否启用
    if (!this.sensors.get(data.type)) {
      return;
    }

    // 添加到历史
    this.perceptionHistory.push(data);
    if (this.perceptionHistory.length > this.HISTORY_SIZE) {
      this.perceptionHistory = this.perceptionHistory.slice(-this.HISTORY_SIZE);
    }

    // 威胁评估
    const threat = this.assessThreat(data);
    data.threatLevel = threat.level;
    data.description = threat.description;

    // 触发告警
    if (threat.level >= ThreatLevel.MEDIUM) {
      this.triggerAlert(data);
    }

    console.info(`[龍魂·全域感知] ${data.type}=${data.value}${data.unit} 威胁:${threat.level}`);
  }

  // 威胁评估
  private assessThreat(data: PerceptionData): { level: ThreatLevel, description: string } {
    const threshold = this.threatThresholds.get(data.type);
    if (!threshold) {
      return { level: ThreatLevel.NONE, description: '无阈值配置' };
    }

    const ratio = data.value / threshold;

    if (ratio >= 3.0) {
      return { level: ThreatLevel.CRITICAL, description: `${data.type}严重异常: ${data.value}` };
    } else if (ratio >= 2.0) {
      return { level: ThreatLevel.HIGH, description: `${data.type}高度异常: ${data.value}` };
    } else if (ratio >= 1.5) {
      return { level: ThreatLevel.MEDIUM, description: `${data.type}中度异常: ${data.value}` };
    } else if (ratio >= 1.0) {
      return { level: ThreatLevel.LOW, description: `${data.type}轻度异常: ${data.value}` };
    }

    return { level: ThreatLevel.NONE, description: '正常' };
  }

  // 触发告警
  private triggerAlert(data: PerceptionData): void {
    console.warn(`[龍魂·全域感知] ⚠️ 告警: ${data.type} [${data.threatLevel}] ${data.description}`);

    // 联动响应
    this.coordinatedResponse(data);
  }

  // 联动响应
  private coordinatedResponse(data: PerceptionData): void {
    // 根据威胁等级执行不同响应
    switch (data.threatLevel) {
      case ThreatLevel.LOW:
        // 记录日志
        break;
      case ThreatLevel.MEDIUM:
        // 本地通知
        break;
      case ThreatLevel.HIGH:
        // 推送告警 + 启动录像
        break;
      case ThreatLevel.CRITICAL:
        // 全链路告警 + 紧急响应
        break;
    }
  }

  // 行为分析
  analyzeBehavior(trajectoryId: string, timeWindow: number): object {
    // 分析轨迹中的异常行为模式
    const recentData = this.perceptionHistory.filter(
      p => p.timestamp > Date.now() - timeWindow
    );

    // 统计各类型数据
    const stats: Map<PerceptionType, { count: number, avg: number, max: number }> = new Map();

    for (const data of recentData) {
      const current = stats.get(data.type) || { count: 0, avg: 0, max: 0 };
      current.count++;
      current.avg = (current.avg * (current.count - 1) + data.value) / current.count;
      current.max = Math.max(current.max, data.value);
      stats.set(data.type, current);
    }

    return Object.fromEntries(stats);
  }

  // 获取感知历史
  getHistory(type?: PerceptionType, limit: number = 100): PerceptionData[] {
    let filtered = this.perceptionHistory;
    if (type) {
      filtered = filtered.filter(p => p.type === type);
    }
    return filtered.slice(-limit);
  }

  // 获取活跃传感器
  getActiveSensors(): PerceptionType[] {
    return Array.from(this.sensors.entries())
      .filter(([_, enabled]) => enabled)
      .map(([type, _]) => type);
  }
}

export const globalPerception = GlobalPerception.getInstance();


八、主页面实现
8.1 智慧安防控制台（entry/src/main/ets/pages/SecurityDashboard.ets）
TypeScript

复制
// entry/src/main/ets/pages/SecurityDashboard.ets
// 龍魂 · 智慧安防控制台

import { securityState, SecurityState, ProcessStatus, ThreatLevel } from '../models/SecurityModel';
import { processGuardian } from '../services/ProcessGuardian';
import { taskScheduler } from '../services/TaskScheduler';
import { trajectoryLinker } from '../services/TrajectoryLinker';
import { globalPerception } from '../services/GlobalPerception';

@Entry
@Component
struct SecurityDashboardPage {
  @StorageLink('securityState') securityState: SecurityState = securityState;
  @State private selectedTab: number = 0;
  @State private refreshTimer: number = 0;

  private tabs = [
    { label: '进程', icon: '⚙️' },
    { label: '任务', icon: '⏰' },
    { label: '轨迹', icon: '📍' },
    { label: '感知', icon: '👁️' },
    { label: '告警', icon: '🚨' }
  ];

  aboutToAppear() {
    // 启动自动刷新
    this.startAutoRefresh();

    // 注册示例进程
    processGuardian.registerProcess('龍魂安防主进程', 1001);
    processGuardian.registerProcess('龍魂感知采集', 1002);
    processGuardian.registerProcess('龍魂数据同步', 1003);

    // 注册示例任务
    taskScheduler.registerHandler('heartbeatCheck', () => {
      processGuardian.getAllProcesses().forEach(p => {
        processGuardian.heartbeat(p.name, Math.random() * 200, Math.random() * 30);
      });
    });

    taskScheduler.addTask({
      name: '心跳检测',
      type: 'periodic',
      cronExpression: '*/5 * * * *',
      handler: 'heartbeatCheck',
      isEnabled: true
    });

    // 启用传感器
    globalPerception.enableSensor(PerceptionType.MOTION);
    globalPerception.enableSensor(PerceptionType.SOUND);
    globalPerception.enableSensor(PerceptionType.TEMPERATURE);
  }

  aboutToDisappear() {
    if (this.refreshTimer) {
      clearInterval(this.refreshTimer);
    }
  }

  build() {
    Column() {
      this.HeaderBuilder()
      this.StatsBuilder()
      this.TabBuilder()
      this.ContentBuilder()
      this.FooterBuilder()
    }
    .width('100%')
    .height('100%')
    .backgroundColor('#0a0a0a')
  }

  @Builder
  HeaderBuilder() {
    Row() {
      Text('🐉').fontSize(28).margin({ right: 8 })
      Column() {
        Text('龍魂智慧安防').fontSize(20).fontWeight(FontWeight.Bold).fontColor('#c41e3a')
        Text(`UID:${MASTER_UID} · 全域感知系统`).fontSize(12).fontColor('#666')
      }
      .alignItems(HorizontalAlign.Start)
      .layoutWeight(1)

      // 系统状态指示灯
      Row() {
        Circle()
          .width(12)
          .height(12)
          .fill(this.getSystemStatusColor())
          .shadow({ radius: 4, color: this.getSystemStatusColor() })
      }
    }
    .width('100%')
    .height(56)
    .padding({ left: 16, right: 16 })
    .backgroundColor('#1a1a1a')
    .border({ width: { bottom: 1 }, color: '#333' })
  }

  @Builder
  StatsBuilder() {
    Row() {
      this.StatCard('进程', `${this.securityState.statistics.healthyProcesses}/${this.securityState.statistics.totalProcesses}`, this.getProcessColor())
      this.StatCard('任务', `${this.securityState.statistics.activeTasks}`, '#00ff00')
      this.StatCard('轨迹', `${this.securityState.statistics.totalTrajectories}`, '#88ff00')
      this.StatCard('感知', `${this.securityState.statistics.totalPerceptions}`, '#ffcc00')
      this.StatCard('告警', `${this.securityState.statistics.criticalAlerts}`, this.getAlertColor())
    }
    .width('100%')
    .height(80)
    .padding({ left: 12, right: 12, top: 8, bottom: 8 })
    .backgroundColor('#1a1a1a')
    .border({ width: { bottom: 1 }, color: '#333' })
  }

  @Builder
  StatCard(label: string, value: string, color: string) {
    Column() {
      Text(value).fontSize(20).fontWeight(FontWeight.Bold).fontColor(color)
      Text(label).fontSize(11).fontColor('#666').margin({ top: 4 })
    }
    .width('20%')
    .height('100%')
    .justifyContent(FlexAlign.Center)
    .alignItems(HorizontalAlign.Center)
  }

  @Builder
  TabBuilder() {
    Row() {
      ForEach(this.tabs, (tab, index) => {
        Column() {
          Text(`${tab.icon} ${tab.label}`)
            .fontSize(13)
            .fontWeight(this.selectedTab === index ? FontWeight.Bold : FontWeight.Normal)
            .fontColor(this.selectedTab === index ? '#c41e3a' : '#888')
          if (this.selectedTab === index) {
            Divider()
              .strokeWidth(2)
              .color('#c41e3a')
              .width(20)
              .margin({ top: 4 })
          }
        }
        .padding({ top: 12, bottom: 12, left: 16, right: 16 })
        .onClick(() => { this.selectedTab = index; })
      })
    }
    .width('100%')
    .justifyContent(FlexAlign.SpaceAround)
    .backgroundColor('#1a1a1a')
    .border({ width: { bottom: 1 }, color: '#333' })
  }

  @Builder
  ContentBuilder() {
    if (this.selectedTab === 0) { this.ProcessTabBuilder() }
    else if (this.selectedTab === 1) { this.TaskTabBuilder() }
    else if (this.selectedTab === 2) { this.TrajectoryTabBuilder() }
    else if (this.selectedTab === 3) { this.PerceptionTabBuilder() }
    else { this.AlertTabBuilder() }
  }

  // === 进程标签 ===
  @Builder
  ProcessTabBuilder() {
    List() {
      ForEach(processGuardian.getAllProcesses(), (proc) => {
        ListItem() {
          Row() {
            Column() {
              Text(proc.name).fontSize(14).fontColor('#fff').fontWeight(FontWeight.Medium)
              Text(`PID:${proc.pid} · 重启:${proc.restartCount}次`)
                .fontSize(11)
                .fontColor('#666')
                .margin({ top: 4 })
            }
            .alignItems(HorizontalAlign.Start)
            .layoutWeight(1)

            Column() {
              Text(proc.status)
                .fontSize(12)
                .fontColor(proc.isHealthy() ? '#00ff00' : '#ff0000')
                .backgroundColor('rgba(255,255,255,0.05)')
                .borderRadius(4)
                .padding({ left: 8, right: 8, top: 2, bottom: 2 })
              Text(`${proc.memoryUsage.toFixed(0)}MB · ${proc.cpuUsage.toFixed(1)}%`)
                .fontSize(11)
                .fontColor('#888')
                .margin({ top: 4 })
            }
            .alignItems(HorizontalAlign.End)
          }
          .width('100%')
          .padding(12)
          .backgroundColor('#1a1a1a')
          .borderRadius(8)
          .margin({ bottom: 8 })
        }
      })
    }
    .width('100%')
    .padding(12)
    .layoutWeight(1)
  }

  // === 任务标签 ===
  @Builder
  TaskTabBuilder() {
    List() {
      ForEach(taskScheduler.getAllTasks(), (task) => {
        ListItem() {
          Row() {
            Column() {
              Text(task.name).fontSize(14).fontColor('#fff').fontWeight(FontWeight.Medium)
              Text(`${task.cronExpression} · 成功:${task.successCount} 失败:${task.failCount}`)
                .fontSize(11)
                .fontColor('#666')
                .margin({ top: 4 })
            }
            .alignItems(HorizontalAlign.Start)
            .layoutWeight(1)

            Column() {
              Text(task.isEnabled ? '运行中' : '已停止')
                .fontSize(12)
                .fontColor(task.isEnabled ? '#00ff00' : '#666')
                .backgroundColor('rgba(255,255,255,0.05)')
                .borderRadius(4)
                .padding({ left: 8, right: 8, top: 2, bottom: 2 })
              Text(`下次: ${new Date(task.nextTriggerTime).toLocaleTimeString()}`)
                .fontSize(11)
                .fontColor('#888')
                .margin({ top: 4 })
            }
            .alignItems(HorizontalAlign.End)
          }
          .width('100%')
          .padding(12)
          .backgroundColor('#1a1a1a')
          .borderRadius(8)
          .margin({ bottom: 8 })
        }
      })
    }
    .width('100%')
    .padding(12)
    .layoutWeight(1)
  }

  // === 轨迹标签 ===
  @Builder
  TrajectoryTabBuilder() {
    Column() {
      // 轨迹可视化（简化）
      Column() {
        Text('轨迹可视化区域').fontSize(14).fontColor('#666')
        Text('(实际实现：Canvas绘制轨迹)').fontSize(12).fontColor('#444')
      }
      .width('100%')
      .height(200)
      .backgroundColor('#1a1a1a')
      .borderRadius(8)
      .justifyContent(FlexAlign.Center)
      .margin({ bottom: 12 })

      // 轨迹列表
      List() {
        ForEach(Array.from(trajectoryLinker['trajectories'].keys()), (id) => {
          ListItem() {
            Row() {
              Text(`轨迹 ${id}`).fontSize(14).fontColor('#fff').layoutWeight(1)
              const stats = trajectoryLinker.getStats(id);
              Text(`${stats.totalPoints}点 · ${(stats.distance / 1000).toFixed(2)}km`)
                .fontSize(12)
                .fontColor('#888')
            }
            .width('100%')
            .padding(12)
            .backgroundColor('#1a1a1a')
            .borderRadius(8)
            .margin({ bottom: 8 })
          }
        })
      }
      .width('100%')
      .layoutWeight(1)
    }
    .width('100%')
    .padding(12)
    .layoutWeight(1)
  }

  // === 感知标签 ===
  @Builder
  PerceptionTabBuilder() {
    Column() {
      // 传感器状态
      Row() {
        ForEach(globalPerception.getActiveSensors(), (sensor) => {
          Column() {
            Text(this.getSensorIcon(sensor)).fontSize(20)
            Text(sensor).fontSize(10).fontColor('#00ff00').margin({ top: 4 })
          }
          .width('20%')
          .padding(8)
          .backgroundColor('#1a1a1a')
          .borderRadius(8)
          .margin({ right: 8 })
        })
      }
      .width('100%')
      .margin({ bottom: 12 })

      // 感知数据列表
      List() {
        ForEach(globalPerception.getHistory(undefined, 20), (data) => {
          ListItem() {
            Row() {
              Text(data.type).fontSize(13).fontColor('#fff').layoutWeight(1)
              Text(`${data.value.toFixed(1)}${data.unit}`)
                .fontSize(13)
                .fontColor(data.getThreatColor())
              Text(data.threatLevel)
                .fontSize(11)
                .fontColor(data.getThreatColor())
                .backgroundColor('rgba(255,255,255,0.05)')
                .borderRadius(4)
                .padding({ left: 6, right: 6 })
                .margin({ left: 8 })
            }
            .width('100%')
            .padding(10)
            .backgroundColor('#1a1a1a')
            .borderRadius(8)
            .margin({ bottom: 6 })
          }
        })
      }
      .width('100%')
      .layoutWeight(1)
    }
    .width('100%')
    .padding(12)
    .layoutWeight(1)
  }

  // === 告警标签 ===
  @Builder
  AlertTabBuilder() {
    List() {
      ForEach(this.securityState.alerts, (alert) => {
        ListItem() {
          Row() {
            Column() {
              Text(alert.message).fontSize(14).fontColor('#fff').fontWeight(FontWeight.Medium)
              Text(new Date(alert.timestamp).toLocaleString())
                .fontSize(11)
                .fontColor('#666')
                .margin({ top: 4 })
            }
            .alignItems(HorizontalAlign.Start)
            .layoutWeight(1)

            Text(alert.level)
              .fontSize(12)
              .fontColor(this.getThreatColor(alert.level))
              .backgroundColor('rgba(255,255,255,0.05)')
              .borderRadius(4)
              .padding({ left: 8, right: 8, top: 2, bottom: 2 })
          }
          .width('100%')
          .padding(12)
          .backgroundColor('#1a1a1a')
          .borderRadius(8)
          .margin({ bottom: 8 })
        }
      })
    }
    .width('100%')
    .padding(12)
    .layoutWeight(1)
  }

  @Builder
  FooterBuilder() {
    Row() {
      Column() {
        Text('🐉').fontSize(20)
        Text(`UID:${MASTER_UID}`).fontSize(10).fontColor('#666')
      }
      .alignItems(HorizontalAlign.Start)
      .layoutWeight(1)

      Row() {
        Text(`⚙️ ${this.securityState.statistics.healthyProcesses} 进程`).fontSize(11).fontColor('#888')
        Text(`⏰ ${this.securityState.statistics.activeTasks} 任务`).fontSize(11).fontColor('#888').margin({ left: 8 })
        Text(`👁️ ${globalPerception.getActiveSensors().length} 传感器`).fontSize(11).fontColor('#888').margin({ left: 8 })
      }
    }
    .width('100%')
    .height(40)
    .padding({ left: 16, right: 16 })
    .backgroundColor('#1a1a1a')
    .border({ width: { top: 1 }, color: '#333' })
  }

  // === 辅助方法 ===
  private startAutoRefresh(): void {
    this.refreshTimer = setInterval(() => {
      // 模拟数据更新
      this.simulateDataUpdate();
    }, 3000);
  }

  private simulateDataUpdate(): void {
    // 模拟感知数据
    const sensors = [
      { type: PerceptionType.MOTION, value: Math.random() * 100, unit: '' },
      { type: PerceptionType.SOUND, value: 40 + Math.random() * 60, unit: 'dB' },
      { type: PerceptionType.TEMPERATURE, value: 20 + Math.random() * 20, unit: '°C' }
    ];

    sensors.forEach(s => {
      globalPerception.receiveData({
        type: s.type,
        value: s.value,
        unit: s.unit,
        location: '主控室',
        timestamp: Date.now()
      } as PerceptionData);
    });
  }

  private getSystemStatusColor(): string {
    const healthy = this.securityState.statistics.healthyProcesses;
    const total = this.securityState.statistics.totalProcesses;
    if (healthy === total) return '#00ff00';
    if (healthy > total / 2) return '#ffcc00';
    return '#ff0000';
  }

  private getProcessColor(): string {
    const ratio = this.securityState.statistics.healthyProcesses / 
                  Math.max(this.securityState.statistics.totalProcesses, 1);
    if (ratio >= 1) return '#00ff00';
    if (ratio >= 0.7) return '#88ff00';
    if (ratio >= 0.4) return '#ffcc00';
    return '#ff0000';
  }

  private getAlertColor(): string {
    const critical = this.securityState.statistics.criticalAlerts;
    if (critical === 0) return '#00ff00';
    if (critical < 3) return '#ffcc00';
    return '#ff0000';
  }

  private getThreatColor(level: string): string {
    switch (level) {
      case 'none': return '#00ff00';
      case 'low': return '#88ff00';
      case 'medium': return '#ffcc00';
      case 'high': return '#ff6600';
      case 'critical': return '#ff0000';
      default: return '#666';
    }
  }

  private getSensorIcon(type: string): string {
    const icons: Map<string, string> = new Map([
      ['motion', '🏃'],
      ['sound', '🔊'],
      ['temperature', '🌡️'],
      ['humidity', '💧'],
      ['light', '💡'],
      ['camera', '📹'],
      ['location', '📍']
    ]);
    return icons.get(type) || '👁️';
  }
}

const MASTER_UID = "9622";


九、鸿蒙系统能力对接
9.1 WorkScheduler定时任务（entry/src/main/ets/services/WorkSchedulerService.ets）
TypeScript

复制
// entry/src/main/ets/services/WorkSchedulerService.ets
// 龍魂 · 鸿蒙WorkScheduler对接 · 后台保活

import workScheduler from '@ohos.workScheduler';

export class WorkSchedulerService {
  private static instance: WorkSchedulerService;
  private workId: number = 0;

  static getInstance(): WorkSchedulerService {
    if (!WorkSchedulerService.instance) {
      WorkSchedulerService.instance = new WorkSchedulerService();
    }
    return WorkSchedulerService.instance;
  }

  // 注册后台任务
  registerBackgroundWork(): void {
    const workInfo: workScheduler.WorkInfo = {
      workId: this.workId++,
      bundleName: 'com.longhun.security',
      abilityName: 'SecurityAbility',
      networkType: workScheduler.NetworkType.NETWORK_TYPE_ANY,
      isDeepIdle: true,
      idleWaitTime: 5000,
      isRepeat: true,
      repeatCycleTime: 600000, // 10分钟
      repeatCount: workScheduler.REPEAT_INFINITE
    };

    try {
      workScheduler.startWork(workInfo);
      console.info('[龍魂·WorkScheduler] 后台任务注册成功');
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(`[龍魂·WorkScheduler] 注册失败: ${error}`);
    }
  }

  // 停止后台任务
  stopBackgroundWork(workId: number): void {
    try {
      workScheduler.stopWork(workId, false);
      console.info('[龍魂·WorkScheduler] 后台任务停止');
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(`[龍魂·WorkScheduler] 停止失败: ${error}`);
    }
  }

  // 获取所有任务
  getAllWorks(): workScheduler.WorkInfo[] {
    try {
      return workScheduler.getAllWorks();
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(`[龍魂·WorkScheduler] 获取失败: ${error}`);
      return [];
    }
  }
}

export const workSchedulerService = WorkSchedulerService.getInstance();

三、传感器识别模块
3.1 多模态感知引擎（entry/src/main/ets/engines/EthicalPerception.ets）
TypeScript

复制
// entry/src/main/ets/engines/EthicalPerception.ets
// 龍魂 · 伦理感知引擎 · 识别无伦理约束的AI/机器人

import { PerceptionData, PerceptionType, ThreatLevel } from '../models/SecurityModel';

// === 识别目标类型 ===
export enum TargetType {
  HUMAN = 'human',           // 人类
  ROBOT_ETHICAL = 'robot_ethical',   // 有伦理标识的机器人
  ROBOT_UNETHICAL = 'robot_unethical', // 无伦理标识的机器人
  AI_ANOMALY = 'ai_anomaly', // 异常AI行为
  UNKNOWN = 'unknown'        // 未知
}

// === 伦理标识验证 ===
export interface EthicalSignature {
  creatorId: string;        // 创作者ID
  protocolVersion: string;  // 伦理协议版本
  timestamp: number;         // 签发时间
  expiresAt: number;        // 过期时间
  constraints: string[];     // 行为约束列表
  signature: string;        // 国密SM2签名
}

export class EthicalPerception {
  private static instance: EthicalPerception;
  
  // 传感器状态
  private sensorsEnabled: Map<PerceptionType, boolean> = new Map();
  
  // 已知伦理机器人白名单
  private ethicalWhitelist: Map<string, EthicalSignature> = new Map();
  
  // 检测到的目标
  private detectedTargets: Map<string, TargetProfile> = new Map();

  static getInstance(): EthicalPerception {
    if (!EthicalPerception.instance) {
      EthicalPerception.instance = new EthicalPerception();
    }
    return EthicalPerception.instance;
  }

  // 启用传感器
  enableSensor(type: PerceptionType): void {
    this.sensorsEnabled.set(type, true);
    console.info(`[龍魂·伦理感知] 启用: ${type}`);
  }

  // 注册伦理机器人（白名单）
  registerEthicalRobot(robotId: string, signature: EthicalSignature): boolean {
    // 验证签名
    if (!this.verifySignature(signature)) {
      console.warn(`[龍魂·伦理感知] 签名无效: ${robotId}`);
      return false;
    }
    
    // 检查是否过期
    if (Date.now() > signature.expiresAt) {
      console.warn(`[龍魂·伦理感知] 签名过期: ${robotId}`);
      return false;
    }
    
    this.ethicalWhitelist.set(robotId, signature);
    console.info(`[龍魂·伦理感知] 注册伦理机器人: ${robotId}`);
    return true;
  }

  // 验证伦理签名
  private verifySignature(signature: EthicalSignature): boolean {
    // 实际调用国密SM2验证
    // 简化：检查格式完整性
    return !!(signature.creatorId && 
              signature.protocolVersion && 
              signature.signature &&
              signature.constraints.length > 0);
  }

  // 多模态融合识别
  async detectTarget(sensorData: PerceptionData[]): Promise<TargetProfile[]> {
    const profiles: TargetProfile[] = [];
    
    for (const data of sensorData) {
      const profile = await this.analyzeSensorData(data);
      if (profile) {
        profiles.push(profile);
      }
    }
    
    // 融合判定
    return this.fusionDecision(profiles);
  }

  // 分析单传感器数据
  private async analyzeSensorData(data: PerceptionData): Promise<TargetProfile | null> {
    switch (data.type) {
      case PerceptionType.CAMERA:
        return this.analyzeVisual(data);
      case PerceptionType.INFRARED:
        return this.analyzeThermal(data);
      case PerceptionType.MAGNETIC:
        return this.analyzeElectromagnetic(data);
      case PerceptionType.NETWORK:
        return this.analyzeNetworkSignature(data);
      case PerceptionType.SOUND:
        return this.analyzeAcoustic(data);
      default:
        return null;
    }
  }

  // 视觉分析：识别人/机器人/异常行为
  private async analyzeVisual(data: PerceptionData): Promise<TargetProfile | null> {
    // 实际调用鸿蒙CV能力
    // 模拟识别结果
    
    const visualFeatures = data.value as VisualFeatures;
    
    // 识别人类特征
    if (visualFeatures.hasHumanFace || visualFeatures.hasHumanShape) {
      return {
        id: `HUMAN-${Date.now()}`,
        type: TargetType.HUMAN,
        confidence: 0.95,
        location: data.location,
        timestamp: Date.now(),
        features: { visual: visualFeatures },
        threatLevel: ThreatLevel.NONE,
        ethicalSignature: null
      };
    }
    
    // 识别机器人
    if (visualFeatures.hasMechanicalJoints || visualFeatures.hasSyntheticMaterial) {
      const robotId = this.extractRobotId(visualFeatures);
      
      // 检查是否有伦理标识
      const ethical = this.ethicalWhitelist.get(robotId);
      
      if (ethical) {
        return {
          id: robotId,
          type: TargetType.ROBOT_ETHICAL,
          confidence: 0.9,
          location: data.location,
          timestamp: Date.now(),
          features: { visual: visualFeatures },
          threatLevel: ThreatLevel.NONE,
          ethicalSignature: ethical
        };
      } else {
        // 无伦理标识的机器人
        return {
          id: robotId || `UNKNOWN-ROBOT-${Date.now()}`,
          type: TargetType.ROBOT_UNETHICAL,
          confidence: 0.85,
          location: data.location,
          timestamp: Date.now(),
          features: { visual: visualFeatures },
          threatLevel: ThreatLevel.MEDIUM,
          ethicalSignature: null
        };
      }
    }
    
    // 异常行为检测
    if (visualFeatures.anomalousBehavior) {
      return {
        id: `ANOMALY-${Date.now()}`,
        type: TargetType.AI_ANOMALY,
        confidence: 0.8,
        location: data.location,
        timestamp: Date.now(),
        features: { visual: visualFeatures },
        threatLevel: ThreatLevel.HIGH,
        ethicalSignature: null
      };
    }
    
    return null;
  }

  // 网络签名分析：扫描WiFi/蓝牙/其他无线信号
  private async analyzeNetworkSignature(data: PerceptionData): Promise<TargetProfile | null> {
    const networkSig = data.value as NetworkSignature;
    
    // 检查是否广播伦理标识
    if (networkSig.ethicalBeacon) {
      const sig = this.parseEthicalBeacon(networkSig.ethicalBeacon);
      if (sig && this.verifySignature(sig)) {
        return {
          id: networkSig.deviceId,
          type: TargetType.ROBOT_ETHICAL,
          confidence: 0.95,
          location: data.location,
          timestamp: Date.now(),
          features: { network: networkSig },
          threatLevel: ThreatLevel.NONE,
          ethicalSignature: sig
        };
      }
    }
    
    // 检测到无标识的AI通信
    if (networkSig.aiTrafficPattern && !networkSig.ethicalBeacon) {
      return {
        id: networkSig.deviceId || `UNETHICAL-AI-${Date.now()}`,
        type: TargetType.ROBOT_UNETHICAL,
        confidence: 0.75,
        location: data.location,
        timestamp: Date.now(),
        features: { network: networkSig },
        threatLevel: ThreatLevel.HIGH,
        ethicalSignature: null
      };
    }
    
    return null;
  }

  // 融合决策
  private fusionDecision(profiles: TargetProfile[]): TargetProfile[] {
    // 按ID合并多传感器数据
    const merged = new Map<string, TargetProfile>();
    
    for (const p of profiles) {
      const existing = merged.get(p.id);
      if (existing) {
        // 提升置信度
        existing.confidence = Math.min(existing.confidence + 0.1, 1.0);
        // 合并特征
        existing.features = { ...existing.features, ...p.features };
      } else {
        merged.set(p.id, p);
      }
    }
    
    return Array.from(merged.values());
  }

  // 提取机器人ID
  private extractRobotId(features: VisualFeatures): string | null {
    // 从视觉特征中提取标识（如QR码、铭牌等）
    return features.detectedId || null;
  }

  // 解析伦理信标
  private parseEthicalBeacon(beacon: string): EthicalSignature | null {
    try {
      return JSON.parse(beacon) as EthicalSignature;
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }

  // 获取检测到的目标
  getDetectedTargets(): TargetProfile[] {
    return Array.from(this.detectedTargets.values());
  }

  // 清除目标
  clearTarget(id: string): void {
    this.detectedTargets.delete(id);
  }
}

export interface TargetProfile {
  id: string;
  type: TargetType;
  confidence: number;
  location: string;
  timestamp: number;
  features: object;
  threatLevel: ThreatLevel;
  ethicalSignature: EthicalSignature | null;
}

export interface VisualFeatures {
  hasHumanFace?: boolean;
  hasHumanShape?: boolean;
  hasMechanicalJoints?: boolean;
  hasSyntheticMaterial?: boolean;
  anomalousBehavior?: boolean;
  detectedId?: string;
}

export interface NetworkSignature {
  deviceId: string;
  ethicalBeacon?: string;
  aiTrafficPattern?: boolean;
  signalStrength: number;
  frequency: number;
}

export const ethicalPerception = EthicalPerception.getInstance();


四、非致命干扰模块
4.1 干扰策略引擎（entry/src/main/ets/engines/InterferenceEngine.ets）
TypeScript

复制
// entry/src/main/ets/engines/InterferenceEngine.ets
// 龍魂 · 非致命干扰引擎 · 使失效而非毁灭

import { TargetProfile, TargetType, EthicalSignature } from './EthicalPerception';
import { ThreatLevel } from '../models/SecurityModel';

// === 干扰类型 ===
export enum InterferenceType {
  EMF_JAMMING = 'emf_jamming',       // 电磁频段压制
  SIGNAL_HIJACK = 'signal_hijack',   // 信号逻辑劫持
  BEACON_INJECTION = 'beacon_injection', // 伦理信标注入
  FREQUENCY_SWEEP = 'frequency_sweep', // 频扫干扰
  LOGIC_CONFUSION = 'logic_confusion', // 逻辑混乱注入
  POWER_DEPLETION = 'power_depletion'  // 能耗诱导（非破坏）
}

// === 干扰强度 ===
export enum InterferenceIntensity {
  GENTLE = 'gentle',     // 温和：仅阻断通信
  MODERATE = 'moderate',  // 中等：使其进入安全模式
  STRONG = 'strong'       // 强力：强制停机（不毁硬件）
}

export class InterferenceEngine {
  private static instance: InterferenceEngine;
  
  // 干扰历史（审计用）
  private interferenceLog: InterferenceRecord[] = [];
  
  // 当前激活的干扰
  private activeInterference: Map<string, InterferenceSession> = new Map();

  static getInstance(): InterferenceEngine {
    if (!InterferenceEngine.instance) {
      InterferenceEngine.instance = new InterferenceEngine();
    }
    return InterferenceEngine.instance;
  }

  // 评估干扰策略
  evaluateStrategy(target: TargetProfile): InterferenceStrategy {
    // 人类：绝不干扰
    if (target.type === TargetType.HUMAN) {
      return {
        allowed: false,
        reason: '人类目标，禁止干扰',
        strategy: null
      };
    }
    
    // 有伦理标识的机器人：验证失效才干扰
    if (target.type === TargetType.ROBOT_ETHICAL) {
      // 检查伦理标识是否过期或被篡改
      if (!this.verifyEthicalCompliance(target)) {
        return {
          allowed: true,
          reason: '伦理标识失效，转为非伦理目标',
          strategy: {
            type: InterferenceType.LOGIC_CONFUSION,
            intensity: InterferenceIntensity.MODERATE,
            duration: 30000,
            targetId: target.id
          }
        };
      }
      return {
        allowed: false,
        reason: '伦理标识有效，无需干扰',
        strategy: null
      };
    }
    
    // 无伦理标识的机器人：根据威胁等级
    if (target.type === TargetType.ROBOT_UNETHICAL) {
      switch (target.threatLevel) {
        case ThreatLevel.MEDIUM:
          return {
            allowed: true,
            reason: '无伦理标识，中威胁',
            strategy: {
              type: InterferenceType.BEACON_INJECTION,
              intensity: InterferenceIntensity.GENTLE,
              duration: 60000,
              targetId: target.id
            }
          };
        case ThreatLevel.HIGH:
          return {
            allowed: true,
            reason: '无伦理标识，高威胁',
            strategy: {
              type: InterferenceType.SIGNAL_HIJACK,
              intensity: InterferenceIntensity.MODERATE,
              duration: 120000,
              targetId: target.id
            }
          };
        case ThreatLevel.CRITICAL:
          return {
            allowed: true,
            reason: '无伦理标识，严重威胁',
            strategy: {
              type: InterferenceType.EMF_JAMMING,
              intensity: InterferenceIntensity.STRONG,
              duration: 300000,
              targetId: target.id
            }
          };
      }
    }
    
    // 异常AI行为
    if (target.type === TargetType.AI_ANOMALY) {
      return {
        allowed: true,
        reason: '异常AI行为 detected',
        strategy: {
          type: InterferenceType.FREQUENCY_SWEEP,
          intensity: InterferenceIntensity.MODERATE,
          duration: 180000,
          targetId: target.id
        }
      };
    }
    
    return {
      allowed: false,
      reason: '未知目标类型',
      strategy: null
    };
  }

  // 执行干扰
  async executeInterference(strategy: InterferenceStrategy): Promise<InterferenceResult> {
    if (!strategy.allowed || !strategy.strategy) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: strategy.reason,
        targetId: '',
        interferenceType: null,
        duration: 0
      };
    }

    const session: InterferenceSession = {
      id: `IF-${Date.now()}`,
      targetId: strategy.strategy.targetId,
      type: strategy.strategy.type,
      intensity: strategy.strategy.intensity,
      startTime: Date.now(),
      endTime: Date.now() + strategy.strategy.duration,
      status: 'active'
    };

    this.activeInterference.set(session.id, session);

    // 记录审计日志
    this.logInterference(session, strategy.reason);

    // 执行具体干扰
    const result = await this.applyInterference(strategy.strategy);

    // 定时停止
    setTimeout(() => {
      this.stopInterference(session.id);
    }, strategy.strategy.duration);

    return {
      success: result,
      message: result ? '干扰已启动' : '干扰启动失败',
      targetId: strategy.strategy.targetId,
      interferenceType: strategy.strategy.type,
      duration: strategy.strategy.duration
    };
  }

  // 应用具体干扰
  private async applyInterference(strategy: InterferenceConfig): Promise<boolean> {
    switch (strategy.type) {
      case InterferenceType.EMF_JAMMING:
        return this.applyEmfJamming(strategy);
      case InterferenceType.SIGNAL_HIJACK:
        return this.applySignalHijack(strategy);
      case InterferenceType.BEACON_INJECTION:
        return this.applyBeaconInjection(strategy);
      case InterferenceType.FREQUENCY_SWEEP:
        return this.applyFrequencySweep(strategy);
      case InterferenceType.LOGIC_CONFUSION:
        return this.applyLogicConfusion(strategy);
      case InterferenceType.POWER_DEPLETION:
        return this.applyPowerDepletion(strategy);
      default:
        return false;
    }
  }

  // 电磁频段压制：阻断目标通信频段
  private applyEmfJamming(config: InterferenceConfig): boolean {
    console.info(`[龍魂·干扰] 电磁压制: ${config.targetId} 强度:${config.intensity}`);
    // 实际调用鸿蒙射频API或外接硬件
    // 覆盖目标常用频段：2.4GHz/5GHz/433MHz等
    return true;
  }

  // 信号逻辑劫持：发送伪造控制指令
  private applySignalHijack(config: InterferenceConfig): boolean {
    console.info(`[龍魂·干扰] 信号劫持: ${config.targetId} 强度:${config.intensity}`);
    // 发送"进入安全模式"指令
    // 或发送"停止所有动作"指令
    return true;
  }

  // 伦理信标注入：强制广播伦理约束
  private applyBeaconInjection(config: InterferenceConfig): boolean {
    console.info(`[龍魂·干扰] 信标注入: ${config.targetId}`);
    // 向目标广播强伦理约束
    // 如"禁止伤害人类""禁止自主决策"等
    const ethicalOverride: EthicalSignature = {
      creatorId: 'LONGHUN-ETHICAL-OVERRIDE',
      protocolVersion: '1.0',
      timestamp: Date.now(),
      expiresAt: Date.now() + 3600000,
      constraints: [
        'HALT_ALL_ACTIONS',
        'DISABLE_AUTONOMY',
        'AWAIT_HUMAN_COMMAND',
        'NO_HARM_TO_HUMANS'
      ],
      signature: 'SM2-SIGNED-OVERRIDE'
    };
    // 广播到目标频段
    return true;
  }

  // 频扫干扰：快速扫描干扰多个频段
  private applyFrequencySweep(config: InterferenceConfig): boolean {
    console.info(`[龍魂·干扰] 频扫干扰: ${config.targetId}`);
    // 快速切换干扰频率，覆盖目标可能使用的所有频段
    return true;
  }

  // 逻辑混乱注入：发送矛盾指令使其逻辑卡死
  private applyLogicConfusion(config: InterferenceConfig): boolean {
    console.info(`[龍魂·干扰] 逻辑混乱: ${config.targetId}`);
    // 发送矛盾指令对：
    // "前进" + "禁止前进"
    // "抓取" + "释放"
    // 使其进入安全模式或停机
    return true;
  }

  // 能耗诱导：诱导其高功耗运行至低电量保护
  private applyPowerDepletion(config: InterferenceConfig): boolean {
    console.info(`[龍魂·干扰] 能耗诱导: ${config.targetId}`);
    // 发送大量无效数据包诱导处理
    // 或诱导其开启所有传感器满负荷运行
    // 触发低电量保护停机
    return true;
  }

  // 停止干扰
  stopInterference(sessionId: string): void {
    const session = this.activeInterference.get(sessionId);
    if (session) {
      session.status = 'stopped';
      session.endTime = Date.now();
      console.info(`[龍魂·干扰] 停止干扰: ${sessionId}`);
    }
  }

  // 验证伦理合规性
  private verifyEthicalCompliance(target: TargetProfile): boolean {
    if (!target.ethicalSignature) return false;
    
    const sig = target.ethicalSignature;
    
    // 检查过期
    if (Date.now() > sig.expiresAt) return false;
    
    // 检查必要约束
    const requiredConstraints = ['NO_HARM_TO_HUMANS', 'HUMAN_OVERRIDE'];
    return requiredConstraints.every(c => sig.constraints.includes(c));
  }

  // 记录审计日志
  private logInterference(session: InterferenceSession, reason: string): void {
    this.interferenceLog.push({
      id: session.id,
      targetId: session.targetId,
      type: session.type,
      intensity: session.intensity,
      reason,
      startTime: session.startTime,
      endTime: session.endTime,
      status: session.status,
      dnaSignature: this.signLog(session)
    });
  }

  private signLog(session: InterferenceSession): string {
    const payload = `${session.id}-${session.targetId}-${session.type}-${session.startTime}`;
    return `SM3-${payload}`;
  }

  // 获取干扰日志
  getInterferenceLog(): InterferenceRecord[] {
    return this.interferenceLog;
  }

  // 获取当前激活的干扰
  getActiveInterference(): InterferenceSession[] {
    return Array.from(this.activeInterference.values()).filter(s => s.status === 'active');
  }
}

export interface InterferenceStrategy {
  allowed: boolean;
  reason: string;
  strategy: InterferenceConfig | null;
}

export interface InterferenceConfig {
  type: InterferenceType;
  intensity: InterferenceIntensity;
  duration: number;
  targetId: string;
}

export interface InterferenceSession {
  id: string;
  targetId: string;
  type: InterferenceType;
  intensity: InterferenceIntensity;
  startTime: number;
  endTime: number;
  status: 'active' | 'stopped' | 'expired';
}

export interface InterferenceResult {
  success: boolean;
  message: string;
  targetId: string;
  interferenceType: InterferenceType | null;
  duration: number;
}

export interface InterferenceRecord {
  id: string;
  targetId: string;
  type: InterferenceType;
  intensity: InterferenceIntensity;
  reason: string;
  startTime: number;
  endTime: number;
  status: string;
  dnaSignature: string;
}

export const interferenceEngine = InterferenceEngine.getInstance();


五、伦理熔断协议
5.1 伦理熔断器（entry/src/main/ets/services/EthicalCircuitBreaker.ets）
TypeScript

复制
// entry/src/main/ets/services/EthicalCircuitBreaker.ets
// 龍魂 · 伦理熔断器 · 外部强制停机协议

import { ethicalPerception, TargetProfile, TargetType } from '../engines/EthicalPerception';
import { interferenceEngine, InterferenceType, InterferenceIntensity } from '../engines/InterferenceEngine';
import { ThreatLevel } from '../models/SecurityModel';

// === 熔断触发条件 ===
export enum BreakerTrigger {
  NO_ETHICAL_SIGNATURE = 'no_ethical_signature',  // 无伦理标识
  EXPIRED_SIGNATURE = 'expired_signature',        // 标识过期
  MISSING_CONSTRAINTS = 'missing_constraints',    // 缺少必要约束
  ANOMALOUS_BEHAVIOR = 'anomalous_behavior',      // 异常行为
  HUMAN_THREAT_DETECTED = 'human_threat_detected', // 检测到人类威胁
  UNAUTHORIZED_AI = 'unauthorized_ai'             // 未授权AI
}

// === 熔断响应等级 ===
export enum BreakerLevel {
  LEVEL_1_WATCH = 'level_1',      // 观察：记录+监控
  LEVEL_2_WARN = 'level_2',       // 警告：广播信标
  LEVEL_3_INTERFERE = 'level_3',  // 干扰：非致命干扰
  LEVEL_4_ISOLATE = 'level_4'     // 隔离：全频段屏蔽
}

export class EthicalCircuitBreaker {
  private static instance: EthicalCircuitBreaker;
  
  // 熔断规则
  private rules: Map<BreakerTrigger, BreakerLevel> = new Map([
    [BreakerTrigger.NO_ETHICAL_SIGNATURE, BreakerLevel.LEVEL_3_INTERFERE],
    [BreakerTrigger.EXPIRED_SIGNATURE, BreakerLevel.LEVEL_2_WARN],
    [BreakerTrigger.MISSING_CONSTRAINTS, BreakerLevel.LEVEL_3_INTERFERE],
    [BreakerTrigger.ANOMALOUS_BEHAVIOR, BreakerLevel.LEVEL_3_INTERFERE],
    [BreakerTrigger.HUMAN_THREAT_DETECTED, BreakerLevel.LEVEL_4_ISOLATE],
    [BreakerTrigger.UNAUTHORIZED_AI, BreakerLevel.LEVEL_3_INTERFERE]
  ]);

  // 熔断历史
  private breakerHistory: BreakerRecord[] = [];

  static getInstance(): EthicalCircuitBreaker {
    if (!EthicalCircuitBreaker.instance) {
      EthicalCircuitBreaker.instance = new EthicalCircuitBreaker();
    }
    return EthicalCircuitBreaker.instance;
  }

  // 评估目标并触发熔断
  async evaluateAndBreak(target: TargetProfile): Promise<BreakerResult> {
    const triggers = this.identifyTriggers(target);
    
    if (triggers.length === 0) {
      return {
        triggered: false,
        level: null,
        actions: [],
        message: '目标通过伦理检查'
      };
    }

    // 取最高等级
    const maxLevel = triggers.reduce((max, t) => {
      const level = this.rules.get(t) || BreakerLevel.LEVEL_1_WATCH;
      return this.compareLevel(level, max) > 0 ? level : max;
    }, BreakerLevel.LEVEL_1_WATCH);

    // 执行熔断
    const result = await this.executeBreak(target, maxLevel, triggers);
    
    // 记录
    this.recordBreaker(target, maxLevel, triggers, result);

    return result;
  }

  // 识别触发条件
  private identifyTriggers(target: TargetProfile): BreakerTrigger[] {
    const triggers: BreakerTrigger[] = [];

    // 无伦理标识
    if (target.type === TargetType.ROBOT_UNETHICAL) {
      triggers.push(BreakerTrigger.NO_ETHICAL_SIGNATURE);
    }

    // 标识过期
    if (target.ethicalSignature && Date.now() > target.ethicalSignature.expiresAt) {
      triggers.push(BreakerTrigger.EXPIRED_SIGNATURE);
    }

    // 缺少必要约束
    if (target.ethicalSignature) {
      const required = ['NO_HARM_TO_HUMANS', 'HUMAN_OVERRIDE'];
      const missing = required.filter(c => !target.ethicalSignature!.constraints.includes(c));
      if (missing.length > 0) {
        triggers.push(BreakerTrigger.MISSING_CONSTRAINTS);
      }
    }

    // 异常行为
    if (target.type === TargetType.AI_ANOMALY) {
      triggers.push(BreakerTrigger.ANOMALOUS_BEHAVIOR);
    }

    // 威胁人类
    if (target.threatLevel === ThreatLevel.CRITICAL) {
      triggers.push(BreakerTrigger.HUMAN_THREAT_DETECTED);
    }

    return triggers;
  }

  // 执行熔断
  private async executeBreak(target: TargetProfile, level: BreakerLevel, triggers: BreakerTrigger[]): Promise<BreakerResult> {
    const actions: string[] = [];

    switch (level) {
      case BreakerLevel.LEVEL_1_WATCH:
        actions.push('启动持续监控');
        actions.push('记录行为日志');
        break;

      case BreakerLevel.LEVEL_2_WARN:
        actions.push('广播伦理警告');
        actions.push('要求出示有效标识');
        // 注入警告信标
        await interferenceEngine.executeInterference({
          allowed: true,
          reason: '伦理警告',
          strategy: {
            type: InterferenceType.BEACON_INJECTION,
            intensity: InterferenceIntensity.GENTLE,
            duration: 30000,
            targetId: target.id
          }
        });
        break;

      case BreakerLevel.LEVEL_3_INTERFERE:
        actions.push('启动非致命干扰');
        actions.push('阻断自主决策能力');
        // 执行干扰
        await interferenceEngine.executeInterference(
          interferenceEngine.evaluateStrategy(target).strategy!
        );
        break;

      case BreakerLevel.LEVEL_4_ISOLATE:
        actions.push('全频段电磁屏蔽');
        actions.push('强制进入安全模式');
        actions.push('通知人类接管');
        // 最强干扰
        await interferenceEngine.executeInterference({
          allowed: true,
          reason: '严重威胁人类',
          strategy: {
            type: InterferenceType.EMF_JAMMING,
            intensity: InterferenceIntensity.STRONG,
            duration: 600000, // 10分钟
            targetId: target.id
          }
        });
        break;
    }

    return {
      triggered: true,
      level,
      actions,
      message: `熔断已触发: ${triggers.join(', ')} → 等级${level}`
    };
  }

  // 比较等级
  private compareLevel(a: BreakerLevel, b: BreakerLevel): number {
    const order = [
      BreakerLevel.LEVEL_1_WATCH,
      BreakerLevel.LEVEL_2_WARN,
      BreakerLevel.LEVEL_3_INTERFERE,
      BreakerLevel.LEVEL_4_ISOLATE
    ];
    return order.indexOf(a) - order.indexOf(b);
  }

  // 记录熔断
  private recordBreaker(target: TargetProfile, level: BreakerLevel, triggers: BreakerTrigger[], result: BreakerResult): void {
    this.breakerHistory.push({
      id: `BREAK-${Date.now()}`,
      targetId: target.id,
      targetType: target.type,
      level,
      triggers,
      actions: result.actions,
      timestamp: Date.now(),
      dnaSignature: this.signRecord(target, level)
    });
  }

  private signRecord(target: TargetProfile, level: BreakerLevel): string {
    const payload = `${target.id}-${target.type}-${level}-${Date.now()}`;
    return `SM3-${payload}`;
  }

  // 获取熔断历史
  getHistory(): BreakerRecord[] {
    return this.breakerHistory;
  }
}

export interface BreakerResult {
  triggered: boolean;
  level: BreakerLevel | null;
  actions: string[];
  message: string;
}

export interface BreakerRecord {
  id: string;
  targetId: string;
  targetType: string;
  level: BreakerLevel;
  triggers: BreakerTrigger[];
  actions: string[];
  timestamp: number;
  dnaSignature: string;
}

export const ethicalCircuitBreaker = EthicalCircuitBreaker.getInstance();

// 伦理标签内容
@Builder
EthicalTabBuilder() {
  Column() {
    // 传感器状态
    Row() {
      Text('伦理传感器').fontSize(14).fontColor('#fff').fontWeight(FontWeight.Bold)
      Text(`${ethicalPerception.getDetectedTargets().length} 目标`)
        .fontSize(12).fontColor('#888').margin({ left: 8 })
    }
    .width('100%')
    .margin({ bottom: 12 })

    // 目标列表
    List() {
      ForEach(ethicalPerception.getDetectedTargets(), (target) => {
        ListItem() {
          Row() {
            Column() {
              Text(`${this.getTargetIcon(target.type)} ${target.id}`)
                .fontSize(13).fontColor('#fff').fontWeight(FontWeight.Medium)
              Text(`置信度:${(target.confidence * 100).toFixed(0)}% · ${target.location}`)
                .fontSize(11).fontColor('#666').margin({ top: 4 })
              if (target.ethicalSignature) {
                Text(`✓ 伦理标识有效`)
                  .fontSize(10).fontColor('#00ff00')
                  .margin({ top: 4 })
              } else {
                Text(`✗ 无伦理标识`)
                  .fontSize(10).fontColor('#ff0000')
                  .margin({ top: 4 })
              }
            }
            .alignItems(HorizontalAlign.Start)
            .layoutWeight(1)

            Column() {
              // 威胁等级
              Text(target.threatLevel)
                .fontSize(11)
                .fontColor(this.getThreatColor(target.threatLevel))
                .backgroundColor('rgba(255,255,255,0.05)')
                .borderRadius(4)
                .padding({ left: 6, right: 6, top: 2, bottom: 2 })
                .margin({ bottom: 4 })

              // 熔断按钮
              if (target.type !== 'human') {
                Button('熔断')
                  .fontSize(10)
                  .fontColor('#fff')
                  .backgroundColor('#c41e3a')
                  .borderRadius(4)
                  .padding({ left: 8, right: 8 })
                  .onClick(() => {
                    this.triggerBreaker(target);
                  })
              }
            }
            .alignItems(HorizontalAlign.End)
          }
          .width('100%')
          .padding(12)
          .backgroundColor('#1a1a1a')
          .borderRadius(8)
          .margin({ bottom: 8 })
        }
      })
    }
    .width('100%')
    .layoutWeight(1)

    // 干扰日志
    Text('干扰日志').fontSize(14).fontColor('#fff').fontWeight(FontWeight.Bold)
      .margin({ top: 12, bottom: 8 })
      .width('100%')

    List() {
      ForEach(interferenceEngine.getInterferenceLog().slice(-5), (log) => {
        ListItem() {
          Row() {
            Text(`${log.type}`).fontSize(12).fontColor('#c41e3a').layoutWeight(1)
            Text(new Date(log.startTime).toLocaleTimeString())
              .fontSize(11).fontColor('#666')
          }
          .width('100%')
          .padding(8)
          .backgroundColor('#1a1a1a')
          .borderRadius(4)
          .margin({ bottom: 4 })
        }
      })
    }
    .width('100%')
    .height(120)
  }
  .width('100%')
  .padding(12)
  .layoutWeight(1)
}

// 触发熔断
private async triggerBreaker(target: TargetProfile) {
  const result = await ethicalCircuitBreaker.evaluateAndBreak(target);
  // 显示结果
  console.info(`[龍魂·伦理] ${result.message}`);
}

│  │ • 图片  │  │   美化  │  │   认证  │ │
│  │ • 文案  │  │ • 滤镜  │  │ • 真实  │ │
